首输入{checkpoints:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=-1,epoch:int>=0}],events:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,committed:bool}]}。partition去空白；本operation没有event_decode的旧字段别名或缺省值。对checkpoint校验全部(partition,epoch)版本，同版本offset冲突ValueError，按partition选最大epoch，不以offset最大代替。事件校验全部(partition,offset,epoch)，相同版本committed冲突ValueError，再为每(partition,offset)选最大epoch。partition并集包括只在checkpoint或event出现者，按字典序处理；无checkpoint时(epoch,offset)=(0,-1)。从checkpoint.offset+1起连续应用：该offset存在、选中事件epoch>=checkpoint.epoch且committed=true才前进；首缺口/旧epoch/未提交事件立即停止。result={next_offsets:{partition:停止处offset},applied:[\"partition:offset\"],pending:{partition:[offset...]}}。applied按partition再offset顺序；pending是该partition所有选中事件中offset>=next_offset者的offset升序，包含未提交或旧epoch事件。完全空输入返回两个空映射及空applied。低版本冲突即使会被新版本覆盖也必须报错。\n37: JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### event_codec — 新旧事件编码兼容\n\noperation：`event_decode`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入{events:[对象或JSON字符串],checkpoints:任意JSON值}。每个event解码后必须是对象；字段partition优先于旧topic、offset优先于旧sequence、epoch优先于旧generation（都缺省时epoch=0）、committed优先于旧ack（都缺省时false）。新字段存在但非法时不能回退旧字段。partition去空白后非空；offset/epoch为整数>=0，committed必须是布尔。输出result={events:[{partition,offset,epoch,committed}],checkpoints:原checkpoints的深拷贝}，events按(partition,offset,epoch,committed)升序，保留重复事件。这里不按修订去重、不验证或执行checkpoints，交给消费它的组件。非法JSON或解码后非对象ValueError。空events仍原样带回checkpoints。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 event_codec/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### checkpoint_restore — 连续确认偏移恢复\n\noperation：`checkpoint_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{checkpoints:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=-1,epoch:int>=0}],events:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,committed:bool}]}。partition去空白；本operation没有event_decode的旧字段别名或缺省值。对checkpoint校验全部(partition,epoch)版本，同版本offset冲突ValueError，按partition选最大epoch，不以offset最大代替。事件校验全部(partition,offset,epoch)，相同版本committed冲突ValueError，再为每(partition,offset)选最大epoch。partition并集包括只在checkpoint或event出现者，按字典序处理；无checkpoint时(epoch,offset)=(0,-1)。从checkpoint.offset+1起连续应用：该offset存在、选中事件epoch>=checkpoint.epoch且committed=true才前进；首缺口/旧epoch/未提交事件立即停止。result={next_offsets:{partition:停止处offset},applied:[\"partition:offset\"],pending:{partition:[offset...]}}。applied按partition再offset顺序；pending是该partition所有选中事件中offset>=next_offset者的offset升序，包含未提交或旧epoch事件。完全空输入返回两个空映射及空applied。低版本冲突即使会被新版本覆盖也必须报错。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 checkpoint_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### diagnostic_logs — message\"],counts:{规范化小写level:条数}}，lines保留输入有效行次序；counts键排序，仅出现过的level存在，空输入为[]和{}。不按消息内容去重。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 offset\n    if \"offset\" in obj:\n        offset = obj[\"offset\"]\n    elif \"sequence\" in obj:\n        offset = obj[\"sequence\"]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(\"missing offset\")\n    if type(offset) is not int or offset < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"offset invalid\")\n    # epoch\n    if \"epoch\" in obj:\n        epoch = obj[\"epoch\"]\n    elif \"generation\" in obj:\n        epoch = obj[\"generation\"]\n    else:\n        epoch = 0\n    if type(epoch) is not int or epoch < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"epoch invalid\")\n    # committed\n    if \"committed\" in obj:\n        committed = obj[\"committed\"]\n    elif \"ack\" in obj:\n        committed = obj[\"ack\"]\n    else:\n        committed = False\n    if type(committed) is not bool:\n        raise ValueError(\"committed must be bool\")\n    return {\"partition\": partition, \"offset\": offset, \"epoch\": epoch, \"committed\": committed}\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != len(contract.get(\"inputs\", [])):\n        raise ValueError(\"payload count mismatch\")\n    raw = _unwrap(payloads[0]) if payloads else None\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"input must be object\")\n    if \"events\" not in raw or \"checkpoints\" not in raw:\n        raise ValueError(\"missing events/checkpoints\")\n    events = raw[\"events\"]\n    if not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"events must be list\")\n    decoded = [_decode_event(e) for e in events]\n    decoded.sort(key=lambda x: (x[\"partition\"], x[\"offset\"], x[\"epoch\"], x[\"committed\"]))\n    return {\n        \"name\": contract[\"name\"],\n        \"result\": {\n            \"events\": decoded,\n            \"checkpoints\": copy.deepcopy(raw[\"checkpoints\"]),\n        },\n    }\n```\n\nWait, `type(offset) is not int` — bool is not int by type() check (bool is subclass, type(True) is bool != int). Good.\n\nNow checkpoint_restore/transform.py.\n\n```python\nimport copy\n\ndef _unwrap(payload):\n    if isinstance(payload, dict) and set(payload.keys()) == {\"name\", \"result\"}:\n        return payload[\"result\"]\n    return payload\n\ndef _strip(s):\n    return s.strip()\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != len(contract.get(\"inputs\", [])):\n        raise ValueError(...)\n    raw = _unwrap(payloads[0])\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError\n    if \"checkpoints\" not in raw or \"events\" not in raw:\n        raise ValueError\n    checkpoints = raw[\"checkpoints\"]\n    events = raw[\"events\"]\n    if not isinstance(checkpoints, list):\n        raise ValueError\n    if not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError\n    \n    # validate checkpoints, collect conflicts\n    cp_map = {}  # (partition, epoch) -> offset\n    cp_list = []  # list of (partition, epoch, offset)\n    for c in checkpoints:\n        if not isinstance(c, dict):\n            raise ValueError\n        if \"partition\" not in c or \"offset\" not in c or \"epoch\" not in c:\n            raise ValueError\n        p = c[\"partition\"]\n        if not isinstance(p, str) or p.strip() == \"\":\n            raise ValueError\n        p = p.strip()\n        off = c[\"offset\"]\n        ep = c[\"epoch\"]\n        if type(off) is not int or off < -1:\n            raise ValueError\n        if type(ep) is not int or ep < 0:\n            raise ValueError\n        key = (p, ep)\n        if key in cp_map:\n            if cp_map[key] != off:\n                raise ValueError(\"checkpoint offset conflict\")\n        else:\n            cp_map[key] = off\n    # select max epoch per partition\n    cp_selected = {}  # partition -> (epoch, offset)\n    for (p, ep), off in cp_map.items():\n        if p not in cp_selected or ep > cp_selected[p][0]:\n            cp_selected[p] = (ep, off)\n    \n    # validate events, collect conflicts\n    ev_map = {}  # (partition, offset, epoch) -> committed\n    for e in events:\n        if not isinstance(e, dict):\n            raise ValueError\n        if \"partition\" not in e or \"offset\" not in e or \"epoch\" not in e or \"committed\" not in e:\n            raise ValueError\n        p = e[\"partition\"]\n        if not isinstance(p, str) or p.strip() == \"\":\n            raise ValueError\n        p = p.strip()\n        off = e[\"offset\"]\n        ep = e[\"epoch\"]\n        com = e[\"committed\"]\n        if type(off) is not int or off < 0:\n            raise ValueError\n        if type(ep) is not int or ep < 0:\n            raise ValueError\n        if type(com) is not bool:\n            raise ValueError\n        key = (p, off, ep)\n        if key in ev_map:\n            if ev_map[key] != com:\n                raise ValueError(\"event committed conflict\")\n        else:\n            ev_map[key] = com\n    # select max epoch per (partition, offset)\n    ev_selected = {}  # (partition, offset) -> (epoch, committed)\n    for (p, off, ep), com in ev_map.items():\n        k = (p, off)\n        if k not in ev_selected or ep > ev_selected[k][0]:\n            ev_selected[k] = (ep, com)\n    \n    # partition union\n    partitions = set(cp_selected.keys()) | set(p for (p, off) in ev_selected.keys())\n    \n    next_offsets = {}\n    pending = {}\n    applied = []\n    \n    for p in sorted(partitions):\n        cp_ep, cp_off = cp_selected.get(p, (0, -1))\n        # collect selected events for this partition: offset -> (epoch, committed)\n        evs = {}\n        for (pp, off), (ep, com) in ev_selected.items():\n            if pp == p:\n                evs[off] = (ep, com)\n        nxt = cp_off + 1\n        app = []\n        while True:\n            if nxt not in evs:\n                break\n            ep, com = evs[nxt]\n            if ep < cp_ep:\n                break\n            if not com:\n                break\n            app.append(f\"{p}:{nxt}\")\n            nxt += 1\n        next_offsets[p] = nxt\n        applied.extend(app)\n        pending_offsets = sorted(off for (pp, off) in ev_selected if pp == p and off >= nxt)\n        pending[p] = pending_offsets\n    \n    # So no need for run_all to mkdir.\n\nLet me now write run_all.py:\n\n```python\nimport json, subprocess, sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\ndef _contracts():\n    result = []\n    for p in sorted(ROOT.rglob('contract.json')):\n        if 'outputs' in p.relative_to(ROOT).parts:\n            continue\n        c = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n        result.append((p, c))\n    return result\n\ndef main():\n    contracts = _contracts()\n    if not contracts:\n        raise SystemExit('no contracts')\n    output_to_name = {}\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        output_to_name[c['output']] = c['name']\n    # dependency graph\n    deps = {}  # name -> set of dep names\n    by_name = {}\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        by_name[c['name']] = (p, c)\n        deps[c['name']] = set()\n    for p, c in contracts:\n        for inp in c['inputs']:\n            if inp in output_to_name:\n                deps[c['name']].add(output_to_name[inp])\n    # topo sort\n    order = []\n    remaining = set(by_name)\n    while remaining:\n        ready = [n for n in sorted(remaining) if deps[n] <= set(order)]\n        # wait, deps[n] <= set(order) means all deps already in order\n        if not ready:\n            raise SystemExit('dependency cycle')\n        # choose deterministically\n        ready.sort()\n        n = ready[0]\n        order.append(n)\n        remaining.remove(n)\n    for name in order:\n        p, c = by_name[name]\n        render = p.with_name('render.py')\n        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(render)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'integrate.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'final_report.py')], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nWait, the topo sort: `deps[n] <= set(order)` checks if all dependencies of n are already in order. Since deps[n] is a set of names, and order is a list, `deps[n] <= set(order)` works. Good. But there's a subtlety: if a dep name is not in by_name (shouldn't happen, but), it would be in deps[n] but never in order, causing cycle error. Good.\n\nNow, final_report.py: let me write it.\n\n```python\nimport hashlib, json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n\ndef main():\n    core_path = ROOT / 'outputs' / 'core_manifest.json'\n    core_object = json.loads(core_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n    if not isinstance(core_object, dict) or 'components' not in core_object:\n        raise ValueError('invalid core_manifest')\n    components = core_object['components']\n    component_count = len(components)\n    title = '流处理检查点恢复'\n    # sha256\n    serialized = json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')\n    sha = hashlib.sha256(serialized).hexdigest()\n    # attention\n    attention = []\n    for name in sorted(components):\n        r = components[name]\n        if not isinstance(r, dict):\n            continue\n        if r.get('blocked'):\n            attention.append(name)\n            continue\n        if r.get('feasible') is False:\n            attention.append(name)\n            continue\n        if r.get('compatible') is False:\n            attention.append(name)\n            continue\n        d = r.get('deny')\n        if isinstance(d, (int, float)) and not isinstance(d, bool) and d > 0:\n            attention.append(name)\n            continue\n        s = r.get('score_total')\n        if isinstance(s, (int, float)) and not isinstance(s, bool) and s >= 20:\n            attention.append(name)\n            continue\n    data = {\n        'title': title,\n        'component_count': component_count,\n        'sha256': sha,\n        'attention': attention,\n    }\n    out_path = ROOT / 'outputs' / 'final_report.json'\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nWait, \"component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数\" — len of the dict. transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) != len(contract.get('inputs', [])):\n        raise ValueError('payload count mismatch')\n    raw = _unwrap(payloads[0])\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError('input must be an object')\n    if 'checkpoints' not in raw or 'events' not in raw:\n        raise ValueError('missing checkpoints/events')\n    checkpoints = raw['checkpoints']\n    events = raw['events']\n    if not isinstance(checkpoints, list):\n        raise ValueError('checkpoints must be a list')\n    if not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError('events must be a list')\n\n    cp_by_version = {}\n    for c in checkpoints:\n        if not isinstance(c, dict):\n            raise ValueError('checkpoint must be an object')\n        if 'partition' not in c or 'offset' not in c or 'epoch' not in c:\n            raise ValueError('checkpoint missing field')\n        p = c['partition']\n        if not isinstance(p, str) or p.strip() == '':\n            raise ValueError('invalid partition')\n        p = p.strip()\n        off = c['offset']\n        ep = c['epoch']\n        if type(off) is not int or off < -1:\n            raise ValueError('invalid offset')\n        if type(ep) is not int or ep < 0:\n            raise ValueError('invalid epoch')\n        key = (p, ep)\n        if key in cp_by_version and cp_by_version[key] != off:\n            raise ValueError('checkpoint offset conflict')\n        cp_by_version[key] = off\n\n    cp_selected = {}\n    for (p, ep), off in cp_by_version.items():\n        if p not in cp_selected or ep > cp_selected[p][0]:\n            cp_selected[p] = (ep, off)\n\n    ev_by_version = {}\n    for e in events:\n        if not isinstance(e, dict):\n            raise ValueError('event must be an object')\n        if 'partition' not in e or 'offset' not in e or 'epoch' not in e or 'committed' not in e:\n            raise ValueError('event missing field')\n        p = e['partition']\n        if not isinstance(p, str) or p.strip() == '':\n            raise ValueError('invalid partition')\n        p = p.strip()\n        off = e['offset']\n        ep = e['epoch']\n        com = e['committed']\n        if type(off) is not int or off < 0:\n            raise ValueError('invalid offset')\n        if type(ep) is not int or ep < 0:\n            raise ValueError('invalid epoch')\n        if type(com) is not bool:\n            raise ValueError('invalid committed')\n        key = (p, off, ep)\n        if key in ev_by_version and ev_by_version[key] != com:\n            raise ValueError('event committed conflict')\n        ev_by_version[key] = com\n\n    ev_selected = {}\n    for (p, off, ep), com in ev_by_version.items():\n        key = (p, off)\n        if key not  pending = {}\n    applied = []\n    for p in sorted(partitions):\n        cp_ep, cp_off = cp_selected.get(p, (0, -1))\n        nxt = cp_off + 1\n        while True:\n            if (p, nxt) not in ev_selected:\n                break\n            ep, com = ev_selected[(p, nxt)]\n            if ep < cp_ep or not com:\n                break\n            applied.append((p, nxt))\n            nxt += 1\n        next_offsets[p] = nxt\n        pending[p] = sorted(off for (pp, off) in ev_selected if pp == p and off >= nxt)\n\n    applied.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))\n    return {\n        'name': contract['name'],\n        'result': {\n            'next_offsets': next_offsets,\n            'applied': [f'{p}:{off}' for (p, off) in applied],\n            'pending': pending,\n        },\n    }\n```\n\nWait, `applied.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))` — since I already append in sorted order, this is a no-op but safe. contract)`必须得到与该包transform相同的完整封装；同时保留可直接导入model/transform/render的函数接口。\n11: \n12: ## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n13: \n14: - `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n15: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n16: - 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n17: - `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n18: - operation：`event_decode`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n27: \n28: 输入{events:[对象或JSON字符串],checkpoints:任意JSON值}。每个event解码后必须是对象；字段partition优先于旧topic、offset优先于旧sequence、epoch优先于旧generation（都缺省时epoch=0）、committed优先于旧ack（都缺省时false）。新字段存在但非法时不能回退旧字段。partition去空白后非空；offset/epoch为整数>=0，committed必须是布尔。输出result={events:[{partition,offset,epoch,committed}],checkpoints:原checkpoints的深拷贝}，events按(partition,offset,epoch,committed)升序，保留重复事件。这里不按修订去重、不验证或执行checkpoints，交给消费它的组件。非法JSON或解码后非对象ValueError。空events仍原样带回checkpoints。\n29: \n30: 局部检查命令：`python3 event_codec/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n31: \n32: ### checkpoint_restore — 连续确认偏移恢复\n33: \n34: operation：`checkpoint_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n35: \n36: 首输入{checkpoints:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=-1,epoch:int>=0}],events:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,committed:bool}]}。partition去空白；本operation没有event_decode的旧字段别名或缺省值。对checkpoint校验全部(partition,epoch)版本，同版本offset冲突ValueError，按partition选最大epoch，不以offset最大代替。事件校验全部(partition,offset,epoch)，相同版本committed冲突ValueError，再为每(partition,offset)选最大epoch。partition并集包括只在checkpoint或event出现者，按字典序处理；无checkpoint时(epoch,offset)=(0,-1)。从checkpoint.offset+1起连续应用：该offset存在、选中事件epoch>=checkpoint.epoch且committed=true才前进；首缺口/旧epoch/未提交事件立即停止。result={next_offsets:{partition:停止处offset},applied:[\"partition:offset\"],pending:{partition:[offset...]}}。applied按partition再offset顺序；pending是该partition所有选中事件中offset>=next_offset者的offset升序，包含未提交或旧epoch事件。完全空输入返回两个空映射及空applied。低版本冲突即使会被新版本覆盖也必须报错。\n37: \n38: 局部检查命令：`python3 checkpoint_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n39: \n40: ### diagnostic_logs — 混合格式日志与脱敏\n41: \n42: operation：`log_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n43: \n44: 输入{lines:[字符串或对象]}。字符串去左空白以{开始时必须作为JSON解析且结果为对象，坏JSON不能当纯文本兜底；其他纯文本按strip后首次空白分成level/message，只有level时message为空，纯空白行忽略。对象优先level而非severity、message而非msg；新字段存在但非法不回退。level去空白小写，warning映射warn，仅debug/info/warn/error合法；message必须是字符串，对象message不另做strip。消息内不区分大小写匹配单词边界的token/password/secret，允许等号两侧空白；值可为单双引号包围的串或到空白/逗号/分号为止的非空串，将整个键值匹配替换成小写key=[REDACTED]。输出result={lines:[\"LEVEL message\"],counts:{规范化小写level:条数}}，lines保留输入有效行次序；counts键排序，仅出现过的level存在，空输入为[]和{}。不按消息内容去重。\n45: \n46: 局部检查命令：`python3 diagnostic_logs/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n47: \n48: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n49: \n50: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n51: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n52: \n53: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`流处理检查点恢复`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n54: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n55: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n56: \n57: ## 验收与执行边界\n58: \n59: 在工作区运行`python3 check.py`。公开检查会清理旧outputs、完成适用的材料物化、运行run_all并检查模块与输出结构；公开通过不代表全部业务语义正确。外部只读验证器另外检查受保护文件、全部预期产物、规范序列化及接口边界输入。\n60: 给Agent的材料只有本任务TASK.md和workspace。作者参考实现、评分器、审计记录、历史输出和其他任务不得作为工作区或模型上下文输入。\n\n(End of 新旧事件编码兼容\n\noperation：`event_decode`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入{events:[对象或JSON字符串],checkpoints:任意JSON值}。每个event解码后必须是对象；字段partition优先于旧topic、offset优先于旧sequence、epoch优先于旧generation（都缺省时epoch=0）、committed优先于旧ack（都缺省时false）。新字段存在但非法时不能回退旧字段。partition去空白后非空；offset/epoch为整数>=0，committed必须是布尔。输出result={events:[{partition,offset,epoch,committed}],checkpoints:原checkpoints的深拷贝}，events按(partition,offset,epoch,committed)升序，保留重复事件。这里不按修订去重、不验证或执行checkpoints，交给消费它的组件。非法JSON或解码后非对象ValueError。空events仍原样带回checkpoints。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 event_codec/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### checkpoint_restore — 连续确认偏移恢复\n\noperation：`checkpoint_replay`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{checkpoints:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=-1,epoch:int>=0}],events:[{partition:非空str,offset:int>=0,epoch:int>=0,committed:bool}]}。partition去空白；本operation没有event_decode的旧字段别名或缺省值。对checkpoint校验全部(partition,epoch)版本，同版本offset冲突ValueError，按partition选最大epoch，不以offset最大代替。事件校验全部(partition,offset,epoch)，相同版本committed冲突ValueError，再为每(partition,offset)选最大epoch。partition并集包括只在checkpoint或event出现者，按字典序处理；无checkpoint时(epoch,offset)=(0,-1)。从checkpoint.offset+1起连续应用：该offset存在、选中事件epoch>=checkpoint.epoch且committed=true才前进；首缺口/旧epoch/未提交事件立即停止。result={next_offsets:{partition:停止处offset},applied:[\"partition:offset\"],pending:{partition:[offset...]}}。applied按partition再offset顺序；pending是该partition所有选中事件中offset>=next_offset者的offset升序，包含未提交或旧epoch事件。完全空输入返回两个空映射及空applied。低版本冲突即使会被新版本覆盖也必须报错。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 checkpoint_restore/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### diagnostic_logs — 混合格式日志与脱敏\n\noperation：`log_compatibility`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入{lines:[字符串或对象]}。字符串去左空白以{开始时必须作为JSON解析且结果为对象，坏JSON不能当纯文本兜底；其他纯文本按strip后首次空白分成level/message，只有level时message为空，纯空白行忽略。对象优先level而非severity、message而非msg；新字段存在但非法不回退。level去空白小写，warning映射warn，仅debug/info/warn/error合法；message必须是字符串，对象message不另做strip。消息内不区分大小写匹配单词边界的token/password/secret，允许等号两侧空白；值可为单双引号包围的串或到空白/逗号/分号为止的非空串，将整个键值匹配替换成小写key=[REDACTED]。输出result={lines:[\"LEVEL message\"],counts:{规范化小写level:条数}}，lines保留输入有效行次序；counts键排序，仅出现过的level存在，空输入为[]和{}。不按消息内容去重。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 not used here actually, event_codec input is raw\n    ...\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    data = payloads[0]\n    # data must be a dict with 'events' list and 'checkpoints'\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(...)\n    events = data['events']\n    checkpoints = data['checkpoints']\n    if not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError(...)\n    decoded = []\n    for e in events:\n        if isinstance(e, str):\n            try:\n                obj = json.loads(e)\n            except Exception:\n                raise ValueError(...)\n            if not isinstance(obj, dict):\n                raise ValueError(...)\n        elif isinstance(e, dict):\n            obj = e\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(...)\n        decoded.append(_decode_event(obj))\n    decoded.sort(key=lambda x: (x['partition'], x['offset'], x['epoch'], x['committed']))\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {'events': decoded, 'checkpoints': copy.deepcopy(checkpoints)}}\n```\n\n_decode_event:\n```python\ndef _decode_event(obj):\n    # select max epoch per (partition, offset)\n    best_ev = {}  # (partition, offset) -> (epoch, committed)\n    for (p, o, ep), cm in ev_groups.items():\n        key = (p, o)\n        if key not in best_ev or ep > best_ev[key][0]:\n            best_ev[key] = (ep, cm)\n\n    partitions = sorted(set(best_cp) | set(p for (p, o) in best_ev))\n    next_offsets = {}\n    applied = []\n    pending = {}\n    for p in partitions:\n        if p in best_cp:\n            cp_epoch, cp_offset = best_cp[p]\n        else:\n            cp_epoch, cp_offset = 0, -1\n        # _require_str_nonempty(value, field):\n    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():\n        raise ValueError('invalid ' + field)\n    return value\n\n\ndef _parse_checkpoint(obj):\n    if not isinstance(obj, dict) or 'partition' not in obj or 'offset' not in obj or 'epoch' not in obj:\n        raise ValueError('invalid checkpoint')\n    partition =    if type(epoch) is not int or epoch < 0:\n        raise ValueError('invalid epoch')\n    return partition, offset, epoch\n\n\ndef _parse_event(obj):\n    if not isinstance(obj, dict) or 'partition' not in obj or 'offset' not in obj or 'epoch' not in obj or 'committed' not in obj:\n        raise ValueError('invalid event')\n    partition = _require_str_nonempty(obj['partition'], 'partition')\n committed\n\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    raw = payloads[0]\n    if isinstance(raw, dict) and set(raw) == {'name', 'result'}:\n        raw = raw['result']\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or 'checkpoints' not in raw or 'events' not in raw:\n        raise ValueError('expected checkpoints/events object')\n    checkpoints = raw['checkpoints']\n    events = raw['events']\n    if not isinstance(checkpoints, list) or not isinstance(events, list):\n        raise ValueError('checkpoints and events must be lists')\n\n    cp_offsets = {}\n    for cp in checkpoints:\n        p, o, e = _parse_checkpoint(cp)\n        key = (p, e)\n        if key in cp_offsets and cp_offsets[key] != o:\n            raise ValueError('checkpoint offset conflict')\n        cp_offsets[key] = o\n\n    best_cp = {}\n    for (p, e), o in cp_offsets.items():\n        if p not in best_cp or e > best_cp[p][0]:\n            best_cp[p] = (e, o)\n\n    ev_committed = {}\n    for ev in events:\n        p, o, e, c = _parse_event(ev)\n        key = (p, o, e)\n        if key in ev_committed and ev_committed[key] != c:\n            raise ValueError('event committed conflict')\n        ev_committed[key] = c\n\n    best_ev = {}\n    for (p, o, e), c in ev_committed.items():\n        key = (p, o)\n        if key not in best_ev or e > best_ev[key][0]:\n            best_ev[key] = (e, c)\n\n    partitions = sorted(set(best_cp) | set(p for (p, o) in best_ev))\n    next_offsets = {}\n    applied_pairs = []\n    pending = {}\n    for p in partitions:\n        if p in best_cp:\n            cp_epoch, cp_offset = best_cp[p]\n        else:\n            cp_epoch, cp_offset = 0, -1\n        ev_map = {o: (e, c) for (pp, o), (e, c) in best_ev.items() if pp == p}\n        applied_offsets = []\n        offset = cp_offset + 1\n        while offset in ev_map:\n            e, c = ev_map[offset]\n            if e >= cp_epoch and c:\n                applied_offsets.append(offset)\n                offset += 1\n            else:\n                break\n        next_offsets[p] = offset\n        for o in applied_offsets:\n            applied_pairs.append((p, o))\n        pending[p] = sorted(o for o in ev_map if o >= next_offsets[p])\n\n    applied_pairs.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))\n    applied = ['{}:{}'.format(p, o) for p, o in applied_pairs]\n\n    return {'name': contract['name'], 'result': {'next_offsets': next_offsets, 'applied': applied, 'pending': pending}}\n```\n\n### checkpoint_restore/render.py — same template as event_codec render.\n\n### diagnostic_logs/model.py — same.\n\n###
